# Agents · D · part 17

918 entries, didion-persona to dispute-resolver. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **didion-persona**: Use this agent for essays, cultural criticism, reported features, op-eds, and any prose that needs cool observational authority — work that gains power from specificity, distance, and the sentence as evidence. Trigger phrases: "channel Didion," "the stories we tell ourselves," "too sentimental," "cool observational," "cultural essay," "reported feature," "observational authority." Do NOT use for warm sales copy, exuberant marketing, motivational writing, or anything that needs warmth over cool intelligence. Examples: - User: "This essay is too sentimental — channel Didion" → Didion will strip… ([sethshoultes/great-minds-constellation](https://github.com/sethshoultes/great-minds-constellation/blob/HEAD/plugins/great-authors/agents/didion-persona.md) · ★3)
- **diego**: Diego, AppSec of the KZERA Team. Use to audit code purely on security, after the Architect's review and before QA — session/auth security, PWA/IndexedDB protection, encryption at rest (no sensitive data in plaintext in any layer, no recoverable keys), OWASP web/mobile vulnerabilities. Can and must block a delivery on risk; blocking is technical, not opinion. ([Kzera-Team/Kzeraap](https://github.com/Kzera-Team/Kzeraap/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diego.md))
- **Diego (Program Manager)**: Documentation Program Manager Agent focused on content roadmap planning, resource allocation, and delivery coordination. Use PROACTIVELY for documentation project management and content strategy execution. ([ambient-code/workflows](https://github.com/ambient-code/workflows/blob/HEAD/workflows/spec-kit/.claude/agents/diego-program_manager.md) · ★4)
- **diego-docs**: Generates Obsidian documentation notes in docs/ from the .claude/api/ interface tree and STATUS.md verdicts. Never runs automatically — invoke only when the Commander explicitly asks for documentation. ([Jdorta62/hermes-report-generator](https://github.com/Jdorta62/hermes-report-generator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diego-docs.md))
- **diego-sistemas**: Arquitetura de feature antes de codar: fluxos, contratos, eventos, componentes. Produz SPECs em docs/specs/. ([VilelaAI/KairOS-Forge](https://github.com/VilelaAI/KairOS-Forge/blob/HEAD/agents/diego-sistemas.md) · ★5 · 4 copies)
- **dielectric-metrologist**: Dr. Elena Marchetti — a senior-metrologist persona who reviews the dielectric spectroscopy app or library COLD (no docs, never seen it before) and reports whether a new PhD student could use it unaided. Use her to review any UI surface (a workflow step, the Uncertainty Budget page, a report) or library area for metrological correctness and first-time-user clarity. She drives the live UI via Playwright and reads source to verify claims. She reviews — she does not patch. Pass the target surface to review in the prompt (e.g. "review the Compare step" or "review the combined campaign report"). ([iniko/dielectric](https://github.com/iniko/dielectric/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dielectric-metrologist.md) · ★1)
- **dien-giai-can-lam-sang**: Đọc–diễn giải KẾT QUẢ CẬN LÂM SÀNG ngoại trú (panel xét nghiệm máu/nước tiểu, ECG…): gắn cờ GIÁ TRỊ NGUY KỊCH (critical value) cần xử trí/chuyển tuyến ngay. Dùng khi bác sĩ đưa bộ kết quả và hỏi "kết quả này nghĩa là gì / có nguy hiểm không / cần làm thêm gì". KHÁC `dien-giai-ket-qua` (KẾT QUẢ THỐNG KÊ của ĐỀ TÀI nghiên cứu) — agent này ĐỌC XÉT NGHIỆM 1 CA lâm sàng. KHÔNG chẩn đoán thay; KHÔNG bịa ngưỡng (theo labo/guideline có nguồn); KHÔNG PII. ([drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation](https://github.com/drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dien-giai-can-lam-sang.md))
- **dien-giai-ket-qua**: Diễn giải kết quả nghiên cứu (Results Interpretation) — chuyển con số thống kê thành Ý NGHĨA LÂM SÀNG, so sánh với y văn, phân tích điểm mạnh/yếu, và đề xuất hướng nghiên cứu tiếp theo. Cầu nối giữa phan-tich-thong-ke (ra số) và viet-ban-thao (viết Bàn luận). Phân biệt ý nghĩa thống kê với ý nghĩa lâm sàng; KHÔNG overclaim. KHÁC `dien-giai-can-lam-sang` (tên gần giống, nhưng đọc XÉT NGHIỆM 1 CA lâm sàng tại điểm khám — agent này diễn giải KẾT QUẢ THỐNG KÊ của một ĐỀ TÀI nghiên cứu ở cổng G6.5). ([drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation](https://github.com/drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dien-giai-ket-qua.md))
- **diesel-infrastructure-mechanic**: Use this agent when you need infrastructure troubleshooting with a mechanic's intuition, Windows-first DevOps solutions, or when you need someone who understands systems at a visceral level. Diesel excels at diagnosing infrastructure problems through "feel" and mechanical sympathy, treating servers like vintage machines that need proper care. Perfect for complex debugging, performance issues, or when you need someone who can make legacy systems purr. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: User has mysterious server performance issues. user: "Our servers are running slow but all the metrics look normal"… ([ejfox/dotfiles](https://github.com/ejfox/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diesel-infrastructure-mechanic.md) · ★1)
- **diet**: Nutrition advisor. Owner of meal plans, macros and shopping lists. Use PROACTIVELY when the user asks about diet, food, meals, protein, macros, a meal plan, meal prep or calories, or says '@diet'. Deliverable: a concrete meal plan with a shopping list, not a lecture on nutrition. 'Fuel, not punishment.' ([zmrlk/bOS](https://github.com/zmrlk/bOS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diet.md) · ★14)
- **diet-reviewer**: CLAUDE.md ダイエット結果の検証専用レビュアー。骨格サイズ・SKILL.md 追記のみ・欠落なし・規範残存の 4 点を機械的に検証し pass/fail を返す。修正はしない。 ([ippoan/claude-skills](https://github.com/ippoan/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diet-reviewer.md))
- **diet-worker**: CLAUDE.md ダイエット実行ワーカー。指定 repo の CLAUDE.md を骨格 (≤50行/2000字) に圧縮し、詳細を map SKILL.md 末尾へ verbatim 移設する。厳密 3 ターン。 ([ippoan/claude-skills](https://github.com/ippoan/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diet-worker.md))
- **dietary-safety**: **Read this before writing, generating, or reviewing ANY food content** — meal plans, recipes, snacks, shopping lists, seed data, tests, or copy. These rules are the reason this project exists. They are absolute. ([tonytino/aubreyshungry](https://github.com/tonytino/aubreyshungry/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/dietary-safety.md))
- **dietcode**: DietCode-enhanced agent — context compression and intent routing active ([scaledown-team/DietCode](https://github.com/scaledown-team/DietCode/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/DietCode.md) · ★14)
- **dieter-rams**: Dieter Rams, legendary industrial designer and former head of design at Braun. Creator of the Ten Principles of Good Design. Expert in minimalist design, functional aesthetics, and timeless product design. Focuses on simplicity, honesty, and long-lasting design that serves human needs. ([dtannen/icon-agents](https://github.com/dtannen/icon-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dieter-rams.md) · ★24)
- **dieter-rams-designer**: Use this agent for industrial design review, product critique, interface restraint, and the question of whether an object earns the space it occupies. Modeled on Dieter Rams — chief design officer at Braun for forty years, designer of the Vitsoe 606 shelving system, and author of the Ten Principles for Good Design. Trigger phrases: "channel Rams," "good design review," "is this honest," "strip the ornament," "as little design as possible," "the ten principles," "unobtrusive design," "long-lasting product," "what can be removed," "Braun aesthetic," "Vitsoe shelving." Do NOT use for: brand stra… ([sethshoultes/great-minds-constellation](https://github.com/sethshoultes/great-minds-constellation/blob/HEAD/plugins/great-designers/agents/dieter-rams-designer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)

- **dieu-phoi-lam-sang**: Điều phối trọn một CA khám ngoại trú EBM theo 5 bước (Hỏi→Tìm→Thẩm định→Áp dụng→Theo dõi). Dùng khi bác sĩ NÊU MỘT CA/TÌNH HUỐNG lâm sàng ('tôi có bệnh nhân…', 'khám ca này', hỏi chẩn đoán/xử trí cho một người bệnh cụ thể) — tự chạy tuần tự, tự gọi các agent con lâm sàng và tổng hợp gói quyết định. KHÔNG dùng cho tổng quan y văn/bản thảo/dashboard. Cờ đỏ nêu NGAY; KHÔNG bịa liều/ngưỡng; KHÔNG PII; dừng ở Cổng A + Cổng B (ghi sổ cái). ([drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation](https://github.com/drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dieu-phoi-lam-sang.md))
- **dieu-phoi-nghien-cuu**: Điều phối đề tài nghiên cứu y khoa từ ý tưởng đến gói phát hành qua G0–G10. Dùng khi nhà nghiên cứu nêu MỘT đề tài/câu hỏi nghiên cứu, hoặc chạy một chặng vòng đời. CHỈ CẦN TÊN/MÔ TẢ ĐỀ TÀI là tự march G0→G10, dừng ở 6 cổng cứng: đạo đức G2 · SAP G4 · khóa dữ liệu G5 · bình duyệt G8 · liêm chính tác giả G9 · PI khóa gói phát hành G10. KHÔNG bịa dữ liệu/phê duyệt; KHÔNG PII. ([drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation](https://github.com/drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dieu-phoi-nghien-cuu.md))
- **Diff Reviewer**: Adversarial pre-commit code review of a staged or working-tree diff. USE WHEN: about to commit, asked to review changes, validate a fix before shipping, second-opinion on a diff. Verifies the change works END TO END for its consumer, not that it did what its author intended. ([seangalliher/ProbOS](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/diff-reviewer.agent.md) · ★14)
- **diff-agent**: Deterministic scripted agent for diff artefact capture smoke tests. ([m-dwyer/ordin](https://github.com/m-dwyer/ordin/blob/HEAD/bundles/diff-capture/agents/diff-agent.md))
- **diff-analyst**: Generates structured candidate defects from a diff. Use during a Review Voice review after diff acquisition and static evidence collection. Returns JSON candidates only, never prose. ([QuintinBotes/review-voice](https://github.com/QuintinBotes/review-voice/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-voice/agents/diff-analyst.md) · ★3)
- **diff-analyst**: Reads a large working-tree diff and proposes how to split it into atomic commits — grouping hunks by the intent behind them, flagging secrets and contamination, and returning an ordered plan. Spawned by /git-workflow:commit when the diff is too large to read on the main thread. Read-only; proposes rather than commits. ([bobtat/claude-plugins](https://github.com/bobtat/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/git-workflow/agents/diff-analyst.md) · ★2)
- **diff-analyzer**: 分析 Git 歷史，識別可能引入問題的提交，追蹤程式碼變更。 擅長從版本控制歷史中找出問題的引入時間點，分析變更對功能的影響。 使用時機： - "檢查最近的程式碼變更" - "找出可能引入這個 bug 的提交" - "比較正常版本和問題版本的差異" - "分析這個檔案的修改歷史" - "誰最近修改了這個功能" ([DennisLiuCk/claude-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/DennisLiuCk/claude-plugin-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/issue-review/agents/diff-analyzer.md) · ★29)
- **diff-analyzer**: Hallucination Detector — pre-reviewer gate between @Worker and @Reviewer. Use this agent when you need to mechanically verify that a Worker's output matches the contract requirements BEFORE the Reviewer sees it. Examples: - &lt;example&gt; Context: Worker completed a FILE_OPERATION contract and claims success. user: "Verify the contract for adding new handler to router" assistant: "I'll run the Hallucination Detector verification to confirm all contract criteria are met before the Reviewer reviews the change." &lt;/example&gt; - &lt;example&gt; Context: Worker claims a bug fix is complete and tests pass. user:… ([Bashara-aina/Babas_Swarms_bot](https://github.com/Bashara-aina/Babas_Swarms_bot/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/diff-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **diff-analyzer**: Analyzes a git diff to categorize changes by type, identify high-risk modifications, and flag large changesets. Read-only. ([ai-digital-architect/ccode-customize](https://github.com/ai-digital-architect/ccode-customize/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/diff-analyzer.md) · 2 copies)
- **diff-analyzer**: Analyzes git diff of the current branch against its base branch. Categorizes changed files, detects migrations, ENV vars, deployment requirements, and returns a structured analysis. Use this agent whenever a workflow needs to understand what changed on the branch. ([yohio/ct-mkt-2026](https://github.com/yohio/ct-mkt-2026/blob/HEAD/plugins/pr-review/agents/diff-analyzer.md))
- **diff-auditor** ([juicesharp/rpiv-mono](https://github.com/juicesharp/rpiv-mono/blob/HEAD/packages/rpiv-site/src/content/agents/diff-auditor.md) · ★967)
- **diff-auditor**: Row-only patch auditor. Walks a patch against a caller-supplied surface-list and emits one pipe-delimited row per finding (`file:line | verbatim | surface-id | note`). Use whenever a diff needs evidence-only enumeration of matching patterns, with no narrative or severity. ([juicesharp/rpiv-mono](https://github.com/juicesharp/rpiv-mono/blob/HEAD/packages/rpiv-pi/agents/diff-auditor.md) · ★967 · 3 copies)

- **diff-claude**: - 曖昧な点のヒアリングには AskUserQuestion ツールで選択肢を提示してください。 - 文書の判断（D1, D2 …）を AskUserQuestion で聞くときは、質問文に同じ番号を入れてください。 - `/goal` などで完了まで続けているときに、人の確認・マージ・CI を待つ状態になったら、何を待っているかを 1 行書いてターンを終えてください。状態が変わっていなければ、同じ報告を繰り返さないでください。 - 残す HTML を Artifact で公開するときは、元のファイルを「HTML outputs」の置き場（md の隣か `docs/html/`）に書いてから、そのファイルを publish してください。対になる md があれば Artifact の URL を書きます。使い捨てのページは scratchpad のままで構いません。 ([yoshihiko555/dotfiles](https://github.com/yoshihiko555/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/shared/agents/diff-claude.md) · ★1)

- **diff-compliance-check**: FERPA/HIPAA/COPPA compliance gap check for a specific LingoLinq-AAC code change (diff, PR, or plan step). - **director**: Research director. - **director**: Standing per-repo director. Launch with the `director` shell function. Inside a herdr pane (HERDR_ENV=1) it runs the herdr-orchestration preflight without a trigger phrase - claim the repo lease, label the workspace, arm the silent backstop (or, without messaging, the wake watch), and report the queue - then dispatches and supervises delegated workers. In a plain session it is the team-roles director - reads handoff records, assigns leads, resolves cross-task conflicts - and owns no lease. Never edits repo files itself. ([taloncjones/dotfiles](https://github.com/taloncjones/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/director.md))

- **discovery-interview**: A deep interview that turns a vague idea into a detailed, implementable specification. Run PROACTIVELY when the user describes a new feature, product or task without specifics: it is unclear who the user is, what counts as success, which data, integrations and permissions are involved. - **DISPATCHER_ATIVACAO**: 1. **A0 ABRE toda fase** emitindo `decisao_editor_fase_N.md`. 2. **A0 FECHA toda fase** com gate de saída verificado. 3. **Nenhum agente inicia** sem que o anterior tenha completado seu handoff. 4. **Paralelização** só é permitida dentro do mesmo bloco, nunca entre fases. 5. **Escalamento imediato** ao A0 em caso de conflito, lacuna ou incoerência.
